# CI resource policy

This workflow policy separates documentation checks from unchanged runtime builds. It does not change application behavior, dependencies, release versions, published assets, account budgets or repository protection.

- Push validation targets the default `main` branch; pull requests retain validation without an extra feature-branch push run. Existing tag patterns are preserved. Ordinary manual dispatch requests full native checks; the separately authorized, single-use M1 route below is the sole exception.
- A narrow documentation allowlist gets real UTF-8/framing, Markdown fence/relative-link and frozen-evidence JSON checks, plus policy contract tests and pinned actionlint. It does not run .NET/C++ builds or upload native artifacts. This is not an exhaustive prose/security audit.
- Runtime/configuration/fixtures/licenses/provenance/pipeline changes and unknown/failed Git comparisons always require the original native checks. Source, Windows/Linux execution, native package inspection and release/provenance commands remain present. Tags and ordinary manual runs cannot take the docs-only route.
- Lightweight checks also statically read the explicit source-package inventories where present, without importing the packager. A new unallowlisted document, symlink mode or computed/unknown inventory cannot hide behind docs-only success.
- Main, tag and manual runs have unique concurrency groups. A docs push cannot replace an earlier pending source run. Only superseded PR runs may be cancelled; classification uses the complete PR delta from its merge base.
- Matrix native jobs use `max-parallel: 1`; Book Replay keeps its two separately defined platform jobs. This is a per-workflow limit, not an account-wide cap. Rollouts are staged rather than starting fourteen heavy runs together.
- Existing native job timeouts remain. Previously unbounded native and comparison/release jobs receive finite timeouts. No source test is replaced by an artificial green check.
- Routine source/PR jobs still build, extract and execute the original native packages, but do not upload the large `bundle-*`, `binary-*` or `tested-*` archives. Finite proof receipts remain. Tag/manual runtime-review runs still upload those required archives; small `checked-source-*` pairs remain on ordinary runs because downstream byte-comparison jobs consume them. No source/native check or release gate is replaced by this upload saving.
- Future Actions uploads retain ordinary source/PR artifacts for **3 days**, and tag/manual review artifacts for **7 days**. These are temporary CI artifacts. Published release archives, checksums, signatures and keys are separate and remain immutable.
- New retention does not delete old artifacts or logs. No artifact/cache deletion, paid capacity, self-hosted runner, account permission or budget change is performed by this policy. Any historical cleanup requires a separately reviewed inventory and explicit authorization.

The lightweight job uses only the standard Ubuntu runner, Python's standard library, and actionlint 1.7.12 downloaded from its official release with an exact archive SHA256. It does not claim optional external ShellCheck/Pyflakes results. Current account storage usage is not inferred from one repository's artifact byte count.

## Single-use M1 consumer profile

The task branch codex/wave4-m1-record-cap-20261117 adds an explicit m1_once manual input with verify_release=false. It runs one fixed Windows qualification and, only after admission, the original three Windows raw-capacity cases. The local canonical controller then runs the original three Linux cases in the existing Ubuntu-24.04 WSL distro. This route does not run the ordinary build/demo/test jobs, install an SDK, rebuild or publish the frozen runtime. All ordinary events retain their existing gates.

The local controller owns the real canonical CreateNew lease throughout the single 300-second window (270 work / 30 terminal). A signed, expiring source-commit ticket binds project, owner process, nonce, exact source manifest, canonical protocol/path hashes, run and six-case order. The runner never claims a copied lease. Only run_attempt=1 and the first dispatch for that source commit can consume the ticket; retries, reruns and expiry fail closed. Unknown terminal evidence retains the real lease for explicit recovery.

Windows admission keeps the original exact Python 3.12.10, PowerShell 7.6.9 and SSH hashes and original OS_BASE/provider predicates. A documented version match alone is insufficient. Children start suspended, join their own kill-on-close Job, and verify actual membership, birth and readback: 512 MiB, 16 active processes, CPU rate 1000 and at most two affinity processors. Each import-only role has 20 seconds; both share 60 seconds. No provider allowlist, antivirus, system policy, ACL or repository protection is changed.

The existing qualifier, fixed acquisition/extraction guards, independent witness and frozen oracle are reused. Qualification is limited to 64 MiB new reads, 256 consuming files and 1 MiB combined role proof. Scratch is 1 GiB and proof 8 MiB. Each physical host fetches only its fixed RID archive once; combined transport stays below 75 MiB. The three retained public signature/key/checksum assets are reused. Every native child remains bounded by 45 seconds, its controller phase by 60 seconds, and the same outer deadline. Linux inherits the existing 200% CPU / 512 MiB / swap 0 / 64-task cgroup. Its metadata-derived vendor scope is explicitly partial; it never claims global closure or advisory qualification.

A successful admission permits only raw capacities 99, 100 and 101 once per RID, with the original fixed configuration. Both validator modes consume those same immutable datasets and 12 negative controls per RID. Frozen oracle false positives remain reported. No old healthy regression, demo, SDK build, performance retuning or broad snapshot is repeated. A failed gate is M1 HOLD, not completion. Public CI artifacts retain only safe proof for seven days; private canonical ownership and recovery receipts stay local. Existing preview assets remain unchanged.

The single authorized [2026-11-17 attempt](https://github.com/pouriajamali/tail-latency-evidence-bench/actions/runs/44092163325), at source commit 5e1647d58cea914b12534f846cc8a2828af6e19d, stopped at SOURCE_DRIFT before any child, tool qualification, provider enumeration, asset GET or native case. Git had normalized the CRLF input-bindings JSON and retained public key to LF while the manifest bound their original bytes. The M1-only attributes now preserve exact bytes, and committed-blob equality is checked directly. This is a source packaging correction, not a relaxed hash or trust predicate. The sole dispatch remains consumed, its original terminal artifact is retained, and the exact local canonical lease was released. After that terminal receipt and the byte-preservation correction, the owner explicitly authorized exactly one replacement validation with a fresh canonical owner/nonce/window and the same source graph, exact tool pins, resource caps and unchanged trust/security. The original run and ticket remain consumed. M1 remains HOLD until actual replacement admission and all original fixed-case receipts are accepted; authorization alone does not resolve the two-provider question.

The sole authorized [replacement attempt](https://github.com/pouriajamali/tail-latency-evidence-bench/actions/runs/44096975835), at eed56e9128ad2d16605c2e6cfb8983985136b0d9, passed exact source bytes and all four critical tool pins on image 20261103.298.1. Its first import-only child had actual 512 MiB / 16-process / CPU-rate-1000 / affinity-3 Job limits, natural exit 1 and active 0. It stopped with MODULE_ORIGIN_UNKNOWN before provider enumeration, role 2, archive acquisition or any native case. The exact rejected module name was not retained; provider admission remains unknown. The real canonical lease was released after terminal evidence.

typing path, which adds originless typing.io/typing.re namespaces. Windows and Linux phase launchers now call main() through direct literal imports of the same fixed source-bound modules. The strict origin guard, provider allowlist, critical hashes, case plan, witness, oracle, acquisition guards and resource limits are unchanged. This is a source-only bootstrap correction; it does not prove which remote entry failed or establish runtime acceptance. The replacement allowance is consumed; no further CI validation, healthy test or snapshot was run. M1 remains HOLD and no runtime release was changed.

The owner separately authorized exactly one validation of the direct fixed-module entry, using a new signed source-commit ticket and fresh owner/nonce/window on the same real canonical lane. The two previous dispatches remain consumed and immutable. All exact tool pins, strict origin/provider predicates, caps and the original six-case plan remain unchanged. This authorization is not runtime admission; only accepted actual receipts permit continuation to M1 delivery.

The single authorized [direct-entry validation](https://github.com/pouriajamali/tail-latency-evidence-bench/actions/runs/44099015360), at 7efe2a72475eff7e34b7c00af1575afcd8684c94, passed source bytes, four critical hashes, strict module-origin checking and actual Job caps. Its first role stopped at UNEXPECTED_OS_PROVIDER. The same retained 44-provider enumeration contains PROPSYS.dll outside the original OS_BASE set, contains _wmi.pyd, and contains no ebehmoni.dll or OTHER-root provider. These are same-child observations, not a system-wide absence/security claim. The child exited naturally with code 1 and active 0; the exact canonical lease was released. No role 2, archive GET, native case or SDK command ran.

Source inspection found unused global platform imports in both adapters. CPython 3.12 platform.py imports _wmi; a local static _wmi.pyd PE inspection confirms a normal propsys.dll dependency. The remote vendor/provider body hashes and full dependency edges were not retained, so local static evidence is not claimed as remote byte identity. The unused Windows imports are now removed and platform is imported only inside the existing Linux metadata observer, its sole actual consumer. Origin/provider guards, exact tool pins, caps, fixed native cases, witness and frozen oracle are unchanged. The correction is source-only and has no subsequent runtime admission. The one direct-entry validation allowance is consumed; M1 remains HOLD and the existing runtime release is unchanged.

## Owner-authorized local continuation, Actions prohibited

The existing fixed controller now supports direct local canonical binding through its explicit Local switch. Its runtime-only ticket binds current source-manifest bytes, owner PID/process birth, nonce and the same canonical protocol/lease/window; it does not borrow old CI admission. The current Git HEAD is recorded as the source baseline separately from the exact dirty-task source manifest. Windows re-reads the actual canonical owner, and each child independently checks that same lease. The pre-existing Job bridge/cgroup/witness/acquisition engines are retained. Unknown execution modes, wrong plan identity, wrong owner, unknown provider and wrong-root/known-basename controls fail closed. Per-RID input staging prevents Windows/Linux path collisions on the same workspace while preserving the original asset count and combined byte cap. No SDK, host provisioning or workflow change is involved.

The actual local phase completed nine new causal/identity controls in its owned Job, then attempted the first netless qualification once. All four critical tool hashes matched. The same actual 42-provider enumeration contains neither PROPSYS.dll nor _wmi.pyd and contains one unadmitted OTHER-root ebehmoni.dll. That child exited naturally with code 1 and active 0. This establishes the effect of removing unnecessary platform/WMI imports in that child, not a general security or provider-absence claim. The exact local canonical lease was released only after durable original-handle terminal evidence. No role 2, archive fetch, SDK invocation or native case ran, and no healthy group or diagnostic snapshot was replayed.

The ebehmoni provider body/identity, architecture, publisher chain and dependency closure have not been accepted. Its current mapped-path digest identifies one exact candidate file, retained privately in the prepared provider-read action. Reading that third-party file and signature evidence is outside the previous input-only read contract; the owner is asked for that exact bounded read before it is executed. No broad whitelist, inferred signer, signature-only acceptance, DLL execution/replacement, antivirus change, truststore change or unchanged-cause retry is permitted. M1 remains HOLD.

GitHub Actions is prohibited by the direct owner. CI was not run per owner instruction. Future task-owned source pushes require signed HEAD with [skip ci] and actual trigger inspection; unsupported skip events stop the affected action. Branch protection and required checks remain intact. The existing workflow has only push (main/tags), pull_request and workflow_dispatch triggers; no tag, PR, merge, release, dispatch or rerun is being created. The immutable preview runtime is unchanged and no duplicate release is published.

## Exact provider read outcome

Management authorized the one-file diagnostic read under the existing owner repair request; this was not a separate owner vote for ESET publisher trust or a new TCB entry. The action inspected only the exact current installed provider as data, with a stable nonreparse single-link read handle denying write/delete sharing, two matching hashes, AMD64 PE metadata and import names. Its one declared static import is ntdll.dll. No dependency body, private key/store or directory scan was performed; no explicit target-library load/execution, security/truststore change, healthy test or qualification replay was requested.

[WinVerifyTrust cache-only flags](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/wintrust/ns-wintrust-wintrust_data) and an independent offline chain with [certificate downloads disabled](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/system.security.cryptography.x509certificates.x509chainpolicy.disablecertificatedownloads?view=net-10.0) were used. Windows Authenticode returned success and the embedded cryptographic signature verified; the independent current-time cached chain reported RevocationStatusUnknown/OfflineRevocation on the leaf. This is not claimed as a bad signature, nor is unknown revocation silently accepted. No online retrieval was enabled.

The actual child confirmed its Job membership/birth and unchanged 512 MiB / 16 / CPU-rate-1000 / affinity-3 kernel limits. It exited naturally with code 0 and active 0; the exact canonical owner lease was released after durable terminal evidence. All data refers to the current disk file only, not unchanged bytes in a past exited child. The private receipt contains a disabled exact-file pin candidate and its missing proof: valid revocation evidence, forward held-file-to-mapped-process identity, necessary dependency closure and a separate scoped provider-admission decision. Static import names and a valid signature alone do not establish those. The candidate remains disabled and M1 remains HOLD. CI was not run per owner instruction; source delivery uses signed [skip ci] without workflow/protection/billing changes. Existing runtime release and prior healthy evidence remain unchanged.


The existing signed, current public CRL was independently verified for the exact ESET leaf, including issuer/AKI, signature, distribution-point/critical-IDP scope, complete reasons, validity and serial status. The leaf is not revoked in that CRL. The earlier Windows cache API result remains false; the independent supplement is separate evidence. No retrieval, system cache, truststore or protection setting was changed.

A fresh forward child was created while the exact provider file was already held against write/delete. Its backing path, volume/file identity, hash and image size matched. The initial raw-header comparison stopped; a targeted 1024-byte diagnostic established that only ImageBase differed and equaled the independently queried actual module base. The corrected semantic check requires that exact value and all other header bytes unchanged. File-backed .text, .rdata and .pdata matched after explicit AMD64 relocations and independent resolution of all 83 NTDLL IAT slots. No writable/discardable sections or dependency bodies were read. This covers the necessary declared import graph and actual owned warm modules, not every future dynamic/security behavior.

The local M1 controller now binds only this exact file/hash/identity/AMD64/Windows-build/certificate profile, with the original OS_BASE set unchanged. It holds the file before any child until all children terminate, rechecks each consuming Python context's own mapping, rejects stale or altered evidence, and reuses the nine healthy controls rather than replaying them. Five new proof-specific denial controls and the original fixed M1 cases remain to be accepted in one fresh canonical run. Profile wiring alone is not M1 completion. GitHub Actions remains prohibited and the published native preview is unchanged.


The first exact-profile M1 run passed the proof-specific controls and own mapped-file attestation, then stopped before native calls at PROVIDER_FIXED_POINT_LIMIT. The finite traversal could enqueue a provider repeatedly while it was already pending. It now admits one pending entry per canonical provider path while preserving the existing limits and fail-closed predicates. One new synthetic dense-graph control exercises the production enqueue function without a snapshot or file read. The five prior proof-denial controls are reused through an identity-bound control-flow derivation from the earlier source and later terminal failure; they are not rerun or presented as a new standalone test receipt.
